844 |     Node processName(Name nameNode, boolean asString) {
845 |       if (asString) {

847 |       } else {

===== M0006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1105-1125
Documentation:

Method code:
1105 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1106 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1107 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1108 |       return s;
1109 |     }
1110 | 
1111 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1112 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1113 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1114 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1115 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1116 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1117 |       // crazy.
1118 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1119 |         sb.append(c);
1120 |       } else {
1121 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1122 |       }
1123 |     }
1124 |     return sb.toString();
1125 |   }

===== M0008 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.isBlockBoundary(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 207-237
Documentation:
/**
   * @return true if this node marks the start of a new basic block
   */
Method code:
207 |   private static boolean isBlockBoundary(Node n, Node parent) {
208 |     if (parent != null) {
209 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
210 |         case Token.DO:
211 |         case Token.FOR:
212 |         case Token.TRY:
213 |         case Token.WHILE:
214 |         case Token.WITH:
215 |           // NOTE: TRY has up to 3 child blocks:
216 |           // TRY
217 |           //   BLOCK
218 |           //   BLOCK
219 |           //     CATCH
220 |           //   BLOCK
221 |           // Note that there is an explicit CATCH token but no explicit
222 |           // FINALLY token. For simplicity, we consider each BLOCK
223 |           // a separate basic BLOCK.
224 |           return true;
225 |         case Token.AND:
226 |         case Token.HOOK:
227 |         case Token.IF:
228 |         case Token.OR:
229 |           // The first child of a conditional is not a boundary,
230 |           // but all the rest of the children are.
231 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
232 | 
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     return n.isCase();
237 |   }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1489-1538
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1489 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1490 |     if (type != node.getType()
1491 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1492 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1493 |       return false;
1494 |     }
1495 | 
1496 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1497 |       return false;
1498 |     }
1499 | 
1500 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1501 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1502 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1503 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1504 |         return false;
1505 |       }
1506 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1507 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1508 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1509 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1510 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1511 |           return false;
1512 |         }
1513 |       }
1514 | 
1515 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1516 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1517 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1518 |         return false;
1519 |       }
1520 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1521 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1522 |         return false;
1523 |       }
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     if (recurse) {
1527 |       Node n, n2;
1528 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1529 |            n != null;
1530 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1531 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1532 |           return false;
1533 |         }
1534 |       }
1535 |     }
1536 | 
1537 |     return true;
1538 |   }

543 |         createNativeFunctionTypeWithVarArgs(NO_TYPE, ALL_TYPE);
544 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE, LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
545 | 
546 |     // the 'this' object in the global scope
547 |     FunctionType GLOBAL_THIS_CTOR =
548 |         new FunctionType(this, "global this", null,
549 |             createArrowType(createParameters(false, ALL_TYPE), NUMBER_TYPE),
550 |             null, null, true, true);
551 |     ObjectType GLOBAL_THIS = GLOBAL_THIS_CTOR.getInstanceType();
552 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS, GLOBAL_THIS);
553 | 
554 |     // greatest function type, i.e. (NoType...) -> All
555 |     FunctionType GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE =
556 |         createNativeFunctionTypeWithVarArgs(ALL_TYPE, NO_TYPE);
557 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE,
558 |         GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE);
559 |

561 |     // registerPropertyOnType about the bootstrapping process.
562 |     registerPropertyOnType("prototype", OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE);

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1926-1928
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1926 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1927 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1928 |   }

===== M0021 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 235-266
Documentation:

Method code:
235 |     @Override
236 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
237 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
238 |       if (n.isName()) {
239 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
240 |           return;
241 |         }
242 | 
243 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
244 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top-level variable.
245 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
246 |         if (var != null) {
247 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
248 |         }
249 | 
250 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
251 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
252 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
253 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
254 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
255 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
256 |           if (assertOnChange) {
257 |             String name = n.getString();
258 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
259 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
260 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
261 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
262 |           }
263 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
264 |         }
265 |       }
266 |     }

===== M0026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 785-798
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
785 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
786 |     if (item == null) {
787 |       return null;
788 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
789 |       return item.getNext();
790 |     } else {
791 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
792 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
793 |         return item.chain(result);
794 |       } else {
795 |         return item;
796 |       }
797 |     }
798 |   }

===== M0030 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1252-1263
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1252 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1253 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1254 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1255 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1256 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1257 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1258 |       } else {
1259 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1260 |       }
1261 |     }
1262 |     return builder.build();
1263 |   }

===== M0039 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 929-988
Documentation:

Method code:
929 |   private void toString(
930 |       StringBuilder sb,
931 |       boolean printSource,
932 |       boolean printAnnotations,
933 |       boolean printType) {
934 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
935 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
936 |       sb.append(' ');
937 |       sb.append(getString());
938 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
939 |       sb.append(' ');
940 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
941 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
942 |       // toStringTree.
943 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
944 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
945 |       } else {
946 |         sb.append(first.getString());
947 |       }
948 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
949 |       sb.append(' ');
950 |       sb.append(getDouble());
951 |     }
952 |     if (printSource) {
953 |       int lineno = getLineno();
954 |       if (lineno != -1) {
955 |         sb.append(' ');
956 |         sb.append(lineno);
957 |       }
958 |     }
959 | 
960 |     if (printAnnotations) {
961 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
962 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
963 |         int type = keys[i];
964 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
965 |         sb.append(" [");
966 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
967 |         sb.append(": ");
968 |         String value;
969 |         switch (type) {
970 |           default:
971 |             value = x.toString();
972 |             break;
973 |         }
974 |         sb.append(value);
975 |         sb.append(']');
976 |       }
977 |     }
978 | 
979 |     if (printType) {
980 |       if (jsType != null) {
981 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
982 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
983 |           sb.append(" : ");
984 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
985 |         }
986 |       }
987 |     }
988 |   }

===== M0046 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 884-923
Documentation:

Method code:
884 |     @Override
885 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
886 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
887 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
888 |       }
889 | 
890 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
891 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
892 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
893 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
894 |             reportGetter(el);
895 |             continue;
896 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
897 |             reportSetter(el);
898 |             continue;
899 |           }
900 |         }
901 | 
902 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
903 |         key.setType(Token.STRING_KEY);
904 | 
905 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
906 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
907 |           key.setType(Token.GETTER_DEF);
908 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
909 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
910 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
911 |           }
912 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
913 |           key.setType(Token.SETTER_DEF);
914 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
915 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
916 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
917 |           }
918 |         }
919 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
920 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
921 |       }
922 |       return node;
923 |     }

===== M0048 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalVarReferenceMap.updateGlobalVarReferences(Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>,Node)

Original range: 100-119
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the internal reference map based on the provided parameters. If
   * {@code scriptRoot} is not SCRIPT, it basically replaces the internal map
   * with the new one, otherwise it replaces all the information associated to
   * the given script.
   *
   * @param refMapPatch The reference map result of a
   *     {@link ReferenceCollectingCallback} pass which might be collected from
   *     the whole AST or just a sub-tree associated to a SCRIPT node.
   * @param root AST sub-tree root on which reference collection was done.
   */
Method code:
100 |   void updateGlobalVarReferences(Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>
101 |       refMapPatch, Node root) {
102 |     if (refMap == null || !root.isScript()) {
103 |       resetGlobalVarReferences(refMapPatch);
104 |       return;
105 |     }
106 | 
107 |     InputId inputId = root.getInputId();
108 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
109 |     // Note there are two assumptions here (i) the order of compiler inputs
110 |     // has not changed and (ii) all references are in the order they appear
111 |     // in AST (this is enforced in ReferenceCollectionCallback).
112 |     removeScriptReferences(inputId);
113 |     for (Entry<Var, ReferenceCollection> entry : refMapPatch.entrySet()) {
114 |       Var var = entry.getKey();
115 |       if (var.isGlobal()) {
116 |         replaceReferences(var.getName(), inputId, entry.getValue());
117 |       }
118 |     }
119 |   }

701 |       if (name == null) {
702 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
703 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
704 |           errorReporter.error(
705 |             "unnamed function statement",
706 |             sourceName,
707 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
708 | 
709 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
710 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
711 |         }
712 |         name = new Name();
713 |         name.setIdentifier("");
714 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
715 |       }
716 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
717 |       Node newName = transform(name);
718 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
719 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
720 |         // declaration.
721 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
722 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
723 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
724 |         // same line as the function keyword.
725 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
726 |             functionNode.getLp();
727 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
728 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
729 |       }
730 | 
731 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
732 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
733 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
734 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
735 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
736 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
737 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
738 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
739 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
740 |       if (fnName != null) {
741 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
742 |       } else {
743 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
744 |       }
745 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
746 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
747 | 
748 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
749 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
750 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
751 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
752 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
753 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
754 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
755 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
756 |         } else {
757 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
758 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
759 |           // (an error has already been reported).
760 |           Preconditions.checkState(
761 |               config.isIdeMode
762 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
763 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
764 |         }
765 |       }
766 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
767 | 
768 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
769 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
770 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
771 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
772 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
773 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
774 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
775 |       }
776 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
777 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
778 |      return node;
779 |     }

===== M0053 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1425-1444
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1425 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1426 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1427 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1428 |     }
1429 | 
1430 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1431 |     Node n, n2;
1432 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1433 |          res == null && n != null;
1434 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1435 |       if (node2 == null) {
1436 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1437 |       }
1438 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1439 |       if (res != null) {
1440 |         return res;
1441 |       }
1442 |     }
1443 |     return res;
1444 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType,Node)

Original range: 440-475
Documentation:
/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
440 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
441 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
442 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
443 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
444 |

446 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
447 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
448 |

450 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
451 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
452 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
453 |     }
454 | 
455 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
456 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
457 |       if (superClass != null) {
458 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
459 |       }
460 | 
461 |       if (isInterface()) {
462 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
463 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
464 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
465 |           }
466 |         }
467 |       }
468 |     }
469 |

471 |       clearCachedValues();
472 |     }
473 | 
474 |     return true;

130 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
131 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
132 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
133 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our current Scope logic
134 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
135 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
136 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
137 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
138 |     //   function f() {
139 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
140 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
141 |     //   }
142 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
143 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a separate scope for catch nodes.
144 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
145 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
146 |         .process(externs, root);
147 | 
148 |     FindExposeAnnotations findExposeAnnotations = new FindExposeAnnotations();
149 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
150 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
151 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
152 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
153 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
157 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
158 |     }
159 |   }

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 514-542
Documentation:

Method code:
514 |     @Override
515 |     void endFile() {
516 |       super.endFile();
517 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
518 |         return;
519 |       }
520 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
521 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
522 |         append(";");
523 |         startNewLine();
524 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
525 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
526 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
527 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
528 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
529 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
530 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
531 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
532 |         lineIndex--;
533 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
534 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
535 |         append(";");
536 |         startNewLine();
537 |       } else {
538 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
539 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
540 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
541 |       }
542 |     }

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
88 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
89 |       ComposeWarningsGuard composeGuard = (ComposeWarningsGuard) guard;
90 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {
91 |         this.demoteErrors = composeGuard.demoteErrors;
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
95 |       addGuards(Lists.newArrayList(composeGuard.guards.descendingSet()));
96 |     } else {
97 |       numberOfAdds++;
98 |       orderOfAddition.put(guard, numberOfAdds);
99 |       guards.remove(guard);
100 |       guards.add(guard);
101 |     }
102 |   }

Method code:
190 |     public static boolean isJSIdentifier(String s) {
191 |       int length = s.length();
192 | 
193 |       if (length == 0 || !Character.isJavaIdentifierStart(s.charAt(0)))
194 |         return false;
195 | 
196 |       for (int i = 1; i < length; i++) {
197 |         if (!Character.isJavaIdentifierPart(s.charAt(i))) {
198 |           return false;
199 |         }
200 |       }
201 | 
202 |       return true;
203 |     }

===== M0111 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.isInlinableObject(List<Reference>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 155-253
Documentation:
/**
     * Counts the number of direct (full) references to an object.
     * Specifically, we check for references of the following type:
     * <pre>
     *   x;
     *   x.fn();
     * </pre>
     */
Method code:
155 |     private boolean isInlinableObject(List<Reference> refs) {
156 |       boolean ret = false;
157 |       Set<String> validProperties = Sets.newHashSet();
158 |       for (Reference ref : refs) {
159 |         Node name = ref.getNode();
160 |         Node parent = ref.getParent();
161 |         Node gramps = ref.getGrandparent();
162 | 
163 |         // Ignore most indirect references, like x.y (but not x.y(),
164 |         // since the function referenced by y might reference 'this').
165 |         //
166 |         if (parent.isGetProp()) {
167 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getFirstChild() == name);
168 |           // A call target may be using the object as a 'this' value.
169 |           if (gramps.isCall()
170 |               && gramps.getFirstChild() == parent) {
171 |             return false;
172 |           }
173 | 
174 |           // Deleting a property has different semantics from deleting
175 |           // a variable, so deleted properties should not be inlined.
176 | 
177 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): This pass's object-splitting algorithm has
178 |           // a blind spot. It assumes that if a property isn't defined on an
179 |           // object, then the value is undefined. This is not true, because
180 |           // Object.prototype can have arbitrary properties on it.
181 |           //
182 |           // We short-circuit this problem by bailing out if we see a reference
183 |           // to a property that isn't defined on the object literal. This
184 |           // isn't a perfect algorithm, but it should catch most cases.
185 |           String propName = parent.getLastChild().getString();
186 |           if (!validProperties.contains(propName)) {
187 |             if (NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(parent, gramps)) {
188 |               validProperties.add(propName);
189 |             } else {
190 |               return false;
191 |             }
192 |           }
193 |           continue;
194 |         }
195 | 
196 |         // Only rewrite VAR declarations or simple assignment statements
197 |         if (!isVarOrAssignExprLhs(name)) {
198 |            return false;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         Node val = ref.getAssignedValue();
202 |         if (val == null) {
203 |           // A var with no assignment.
204 |           continue;
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         // We're looking for object literal assignments only.
208 |         if (!val.isObjectLit()) {
209 |           return false;
210 |         }
211 | 
212 |         // Make sure that the value is not self-referential. IOW,
213 |         // disallow things like x = {b: x.a}.
214 |         //
215 |         // TODO: Only exclude unorderable self-referential
216 |         // assignments. i.e. x = {a: x.b, b: x.a} is not orderable,
217 |         // but x = {a: 1, b: x.a} is.
218 |         //
219 |         // Also, ES5 getters/setters aren't handled by this pass.
220 |         for (Node child = val.getFirstChild(); child != null;
221 |              child = child.getNext()) {
222 |           if (child.isGetterDef() ||
223 |               child.isSetterDef()) {
224 |             // ES5 get/set not supported.
225 |             return false;
226 |           }
227 | 
228 |           validProperties.add(child.getString());
229 | 
230 |           Node childVal = child.getFirstChild();
231 |           // Check if childVal is the parent of any of the passed in
232 |           // references, as that is how self-referential assignments
233 |           // will happen.
234 |           for (Reference t : refs) {
235 |             Node refNode = t.getParent();
236 |             while (!NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(refNode)) {
237 |               if (refNode == childVal) {
238 |                 // There's a self-referential assignment
239 |                 return false;
240 |               }
241 |               refNode = refNode.getParent();
242 |             }
243 |           }
244 |         }
245 | 
246 | 
247 |         // We have found an acceptable object literal assignment. As
248 |         // long as there are no other assignments that mess things up,
249 |         // we can inline.
250 |         ret = true;
251 |       }
252 |       return ret;
253 |     }

===== M0117 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0120 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 380-409
Documentation:

Method code:
380 |     @Override
381 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
382 |       switch (n.getType()) {
383 |         case Token.WHILE:
384 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
385 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
386 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
387 |             Node empty = IR.empty();
388 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
389 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
390 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
391 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
392 |           }
393 |           break;
394 | 
395 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
396 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
397 |           break;
398 | 
399 |         case Token.NAME:
400 |         case Token.STRING:
401 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
402 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
403 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
404 |           if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalizedObfuscated()) {
405 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
406 |           }
407 |           break;
408 |       }
409 |     }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0143 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 652-740
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
652 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
653 |     if (this == that) {
654 |       return true;
655 |     }
656 | 
657 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
658 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
659 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
660 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
661 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
662 |         // with everyone.
663 |         return true;
664 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
665 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
666 |         // both unknown.
667 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
668 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
669 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
670 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
671 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
672 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
673 |         return false;
674 |       }
675 |     }
676 | 
677 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
678 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
679 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
680 |     }
681 | 
682 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
683 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
684 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
685 |     }
686 | 
687 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
688 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
689 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
690 |     }
691 | 
692 |     ParameterizedType thisParamType = toMaybeParameterizedType();
693 |     ParameterizedType thatParamType = that.toMaybeParameterizedType();
694 |     if (thisParamType != null || thatParamType != null) {
695 |       // Check if one type is parameterized, but the other is not.
696 |       boolean paramsMatch = false;
697 |       if (thisParamType != null && thatParamType != null) {
698 |         paramsMatch = thisParamType.getParameterType().checkEquivalenceHelper(
699 |             thatParamType.getParameterType(), eqMethod);
700 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY) {
701 |         paramsMatch = false;
702 |       } else {
703 |         // If one of the type parameters is unknown, but the other is not,
704 |         // then we consider these the same for the purposes of data flow
705 |         // and invariance.
706 |         paramsMatch = true;
707 |       }
708 | 
709 |       JSType thisRootType = thisParamType == null ?
710 |           this : thisParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
711 |       JSType thatRootType = thatParamType == null ?
712 |           that : thatParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
713 |       return paramsMatch &&
714 |           thisRootType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatRootType, eqMethod);
715 |     }
716 | 
717 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
718 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
719 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
720 |     }
721 | 
722 |     // Unbox other proxies.
723 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
724 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
725 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
726 |               that, eqMethod);
727 |     }
728 | 
729 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
730 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
731 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
732 |           eqMethod);
733 |     }
734 | 
735 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
736 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
737 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
738 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
739 |     return this == that;
740 |   }

===== M0149 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0151 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1176-1184
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1176 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1177 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1178 |       return -1;
1179 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1180 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1181 |     } else {
1182 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1183 |     }
1184 |   }

===== M0155 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.ReferenceCollection.isWellDefined()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 310-333
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines if the variable for this reference collection is
     * "well-defined." A variable is well-defined if we can prove at
     * compile-time that it's assigned a value before it's used.
     *
     * Notice that if this function returns false, this doesn't imply that the
     * variable is used before it's assigned. It just means that we don't
     * have enough information to make a definitive judgment.
     */
Method code:
310 |     protected boolean isWellDefined() {
311 |       int size = references.size();
312 |       if (size == 0) {
313 |         return false;
314 |       }
315 | 
316 |       // If this is a declaration that does not instantiate the variable,
317 |       // it's not well-defined.
318 |       Reference init = getInitializingReference();
319 |       if (init == null) {
320 |         return false;
321 |       }
322 | 
323 |       Preconditions.checkState(references.get(0).isDeclaration());
324 |       BasicBlock initBlock = init.getBasicBlock();
325 |       for (int i = 1; i < size; i++) {
326 |         if (!initBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(
327 |                 references.get(i).getBasicBlock())) {
328 |           return false;
329 |         }
330 |       }
331 | 
332 |       return true;
333 |     }

===== M0167 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 560-607
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
560 |     private void extractForInitializer(
561 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
562 | 
563 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
564 |         next = c.getNext();
565 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
566 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
567 |         switch (c.getType()) {
568 |           case Token.LABEL:
569 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
570 |             break;
571 |           case Token.FOR:
572 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
573 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
574 |               if (first.isVar()) {
575 |                 // Transform:
576 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
577 |                 // to:
578 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
579 |                 Node newStatement = first;
580 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
581 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
582 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
583 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
584 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
585 |               }
586 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
587 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
588 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
589 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
590 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
591 | 
592 |               Node newStatement;
593 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
594 |               // but are handled differently.
595 |               if (init.isVar()) {
596 |                 newStatement = init;
597 |               } else {
598 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
599 |               }
600 | 
601 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
602 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
603 |             }
604 |             break;
605 |         }
606 |       }
607 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.JSType(JSTypeRegistry,ImmutableList<String>,ImmutableList<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 112-142
Documentation:

Method code:
112 |   JSType(JSTypeRegistry registry, ImmutableList<String> templateKeys,
113 |       ImmutableList<JSType> templatizedTypes) {
114 |     this.registry = registry;
115 | 
116 |     // Do sanity checking on the specified keys and templatized types.
117 |     int keysLength = templateKeys == null ? 0 : templateKeys.size();
118 |     int typesLength = templatizedTypes == null ? 0 : templatizedTypes.size();
119 |     if (typesLength > keysLength) {
120 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(
121 |           "Cannot have more templatized types than template keys");
122 |     } else if (typesLength < keysLength) {
123 |       // If there are fewer templatized types than keys, extend the templatized
124 |       // types list to match the number of keys, using UNKNOWN_TYPE for the
125 |       // unspecified types.
126 |       ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
127 |       if (typesLength > 0) {
128 |         builder.addAll(templatizedTypes);
129 |       }
130 |       for (int i = 0; i < keysLength - typesLength; i++) {
131 |         builder.add(registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE));
132 |       }
133 |       templatizedTypes = builder.build();
134 |     } else if (keysLength == 0 && typesLength == 0) {
135 |       // Ensure that both lists are non-null.
136 |       templateKeys = ImmutableList.of();
137 |       templatizedTypes = ImmutableList.of();
138 |     }
139 | 
140 |     this.templateKeys = templateKeys;
141 |     this.templatizedTypes = templatizedTypes;
142 |   }

===== M0187 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 640-672
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
640 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
641 |       Preconditions.checkState(
642 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
643 |       Node previous = null;
644 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
645 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
646 |       // are already in the right place.
647 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
648 |         previous = current;
649 |         current = current.getNext();
650 |       }
651 | 
652 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
653 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
654 |       while (current != null) {
655 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
656 |         Node next = current.getNext();
657 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
658 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
659 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
660 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
661 | 
662 |           // Read the function at the top of the function body (after any
663 |           // previous declarations).
664 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
665 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
666 |         } else {
667 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.

   * Hoists inputs with the @nocompiler annotation out of the inputs.
   */
Method code:
1449 |   private void hoistNoCompileFiles() {
1450 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1451 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1452 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1453 |

1455 |       if (n == null) {

1462 |         staleInputs = true;
1463 |       }
1464 |     }
1465 | 
1466 |     if (staleInputs) {
1467 |       repartitionInputs();
1468 |     }
1469 |   }

===== M0228 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 490-507
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
490 |     @Override
491 |     void maybeCutLine() {
492 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
493 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
494 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
495 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
496 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
497 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
498 |           prevCutPosition = position;
499 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
500 |           lineIndex++;
501 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
502 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
503 |         } else {
504 |           startNewLine();
505 |         }
506 |       }
507 |     }

===== M0229 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getSortedPropTypes()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 859-873
Documentation:
// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
859 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
860 |     int count = 0;
861 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
862 |       count++;
863 |     }
864 |

867 |       count--;

50 |   CodeGenerator(
51 |       CodeConsumer consumer, Charset outputCharset,
52 |       boolean preferSingleQuotes) {
53 |     cc = consumer;
54 |     if (outputCharset == null || outputCharset == Charsets.US_ASCII) {

56 |       // outside of straight ASCII), then don't use the encoder, but

92 |   public void validateStatement(Node n) {
93 |     switch (n.getType()) {
94 |       case Token.LABEL:
95 |         validateLabel(n);
96 |         return;
97 |       case Token.BLOCK:
98 |         validateBlock(n);
99 |         return;
100 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
101 |         validateFunctionStatement(n);
102 |         return;
103 |       case Token.WITH:
104 |         validateWith(n);
105 |         return;
106 |       case Token.FOR:
107 |         validateFor(n);
108 |         return;
109 |       case Token.WHILE:
110 |         validateWhile(n);
111 |         return;
112 |       case Token.DO:
113 |         validateDo(n);
114 |         return;
115 |       case Token.SWITCH:
116 |         validateSwitch(n);
117 |         return;
118 |       case Token.IF:
119 |         validateIf(n);
120 |         return;
121 |       case Token.VAR:
122 |         validateVar(n);
123 |         return;
124 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
125 |         validateExprStmt(n);
126 |         return;
127 |       case Token.RETURN:
128 |         validateReturn(n);
129 |         return;
130 |       case Token.THROW:
131 |         validateThrow(n);
132 |         return;
133 |       case Token.TRY:
134 |         validateTry(n);
135 |         return;
136 |       case Token.BREAK:
137 |         validateBreak(n);
138 |         return;
139 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
140 |         validateContinue(n);
141 |         return;
142 |       case Token.EMPTY:
143 |         validateChildless(n);
144 |         return;
145 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
146 |         validateChildless(n);
147 |         return;
148 |       default:
149 |         violation("Expected statement but was "
150 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);
151 |     }
152 |   }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not

Documentation:
/**
     * @param index The index into the references array to look for an
     * assigning declaration.
     *
     * This is either the declaration if a value is assigned (such as
     * "var a = 2", "function a()...", "... catch (a)...").
     */
Method code:
357 |     private boolean isInitializingDeclarationAt(int index) {
358 |       Reference maybeInit = references.get(index);
359 |       if (maybeInit.isInitializingDeclaration()) {
360 |         // This is a declaration that represents the initial value.
361 |         // Specifically, var declarations without assignments such as "var a;"
362 |         // are not.
363 |         return true;
364 |       }
365 |       return false;
366 |     }

===== M0263 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1331-1359
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1331 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1332 |     // unknown
1333 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1334 |       return true;
1335 |     }
1336 |     // all type
1337 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1338 |       return true;
1339 |     }
1340 |     // equality
1341 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1342 |       return true;
1343 |     }
1344 |     // unions
1345 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1346 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1347 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1348 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1349 |           return true;
1350 |         }
1351 |       }
1352 |     }
1353 |     // proxy types
1354 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1355 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1356 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1357 |     }
1358 |     return false;
1359 |   }

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3022-3048
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3022 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3023 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3024 |     if (info == null) {
3025 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3026 |       if (parent == null) {
3027 |         return null;
3028 |       }
3029 | 
3030 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3031 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3032 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3033 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3034 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3035 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3036 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3037 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3038 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3039 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3040 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3041 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3042 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3043 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3044 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3045 |       }
3046 |     }
3047 |     return info;
3048 |   }

===== M0273 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.afterExitScope(NodeTraversal,ReferenceMap)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 83-107
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |     @Override
84 |     public void afterExitScope(NodeTraversal t, ReferenceMap referenceMap) {
85 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
86 |         Var v = it.next();
87 | 
88 |         if (isVarInlineForbidden(v)) {
89 |           continue;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         ReferenceCollection referenceInfo = referenceMap.getReferences(v);
93 | 
94 |         if (isInlinableObject(referenceInfo.references)) {
95 |           // Blacklist the object itself, as well as any other values
96 |           // that it refers to, since they will have been moved around.
97 |           staleVars.add(v);
98 | 
99 |           Reference declaration = referenceInfo.references.get(0);
100 |           Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReference();
101 | 
102 |           // Split up the object into individual variables if the object
103 |           // is never referenced directly in full.
104 |           splitObject(v, declaration, init, referenceInfo);
105 |         }
106 |       }
107 |     }

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 273-336
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
273 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
274 |     this.options = options;
275 |     if (errorManager == null) {
276 |       if (outStream == null) {
277 |         setErrorManager(
278 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
279 |       } else {
280 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
281 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
282 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
283 |         setErrorManager(printer);
284 |       }
285 |     }
286 | 
287 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
288 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
289 |       options.checkTypes = true;
290 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
291 |       options.checkTypes = false;
292 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
293 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
294 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
295 |       // parser type warnings.
296 |       options.setWarningLevel(
297 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
298 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
299 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
300 |     }
301 | 
302 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
303 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
304 |       options.setWarningLevel(
305 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
306 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
310 |       options.setWarningLevel(
311 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
312 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
313 |     }
314 | 
315 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
316 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
317 |     guards.add(
318 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
319 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
320 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
321 | 
322 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
323 | 
324 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
325 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
326 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
327 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
328 |     // error off.
329 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
330 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
331 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
332 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
336 |   }
