===== M0278 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-732
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
128 | 
129 |     switch (type) {
130 |       case Token.TRY: {
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");
136 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 | 
138 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
139 |         // isn't a catch block
140 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
141 |         if (catchblock != null) {
142 |           add(catchblock);
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         if (childCount == 3) {
146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }
151 | 
152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {
182 |           add("var ");
183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
189 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
198 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {
202 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
210 |         add("[");
211 |         addArrayList(first);
212 |         add("]");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
216 |         add("(");
217 |         addList(first);
218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.COMMA:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
223 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.NEG: {
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
245 | 
246 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
250 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
251 |         } else {
252 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }
255 | 
256 |         break;
257 |       }
258 | 
259 |       case Token.HOOK: {
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
263 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
264 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
265 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
266 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
267 |         break;
268 |       }
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REGEXP:
271 |         if (!first.isString() ||
272 |             !last.isString()) {
273 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
277 | 
278 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
279 |         if (childCount == 2) {
280 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
281 |         } else {
282 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
283 |           add(regexp);
284 |         }
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
288 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
289 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
290 |         }
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
292 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add("(");
295 |         }
296 | 
297 |         add("function");
298 |         add(first);
299 | 
300 |         add(first.getNext());
301 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
302 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
303 | 
304 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
305 |           add(")");
306 |         }
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
314 | 
315 |         // Get methods are unnamed
316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
317 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
324 |           add("set ");
325 |         }
326 | 
327 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
328 |         String name = n.getString();
329 |         Node fn = first;
330 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
331 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
332 | 
333 |         // Add the property name.
334 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
335 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
336 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
337 |             // Unicode escaped.
338 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
339 |           add(name);
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
342 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
343 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
344 |             cc.addNumber(d);
345 |           } else {
346 |             addJsString(n);
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 | 
350 |         add(parameters);
351 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
355 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
356 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }
359 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
360 |         if (preserveBlock) {
361 |           cc.beginBlock();
362 |         }
363 | 
364 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
366 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
367 |                 !preserveBlock &&
368 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
370 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
372 | 
373 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
374 |           if (c.isVar()) {
375 |             cc.endStatement();
376 |           }
377 | 
378 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
379 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
380 |           }
381 | 
382 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
383 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
385 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
386 |           }
387 |         }
388 |         if (preserveBlock) {
389 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
390 |         }
391 |         break;
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext());
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
406 |           add(")");
407 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),
450 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {
453 |           add("(");
454 |         }
455 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }
459 |         add(".");
460 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {
491 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         cc.addConstant("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         cc.addConstant("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         cc.addConstant("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
632 |         Preconditions.checkState(
633 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
634 |         addJsString(n);
635 |         break;
636 | 
637 |       case Token.STRING:
638 |         Preconditions.checkState(
639 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
640 |         addJsString(n);
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.DELPROP:
644 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
645 |         add("delete ");
646 |         add(first);
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
650 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
651 |         if (needsParens) {
652 |           add("(");
653 |         }
654 |         add("{");
655 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
656 |           if (c != first) {
657 |             cc.listSeparator();
658 |           }
659 | 
660 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
661 |             add(c);
662 |           } else {
663 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
664 |             String key = c.getString();
665 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
666 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
667 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
668 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
669 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
670 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
671 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
672 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
673 |               add(key);
674 |             } else {
675 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
676 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
677 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
678 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
679 |               } else {
680 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
681 |               }
682 |             }
683 |             add(":");
684 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
685 |           }
686 |         }
687 |         add("}");
688 |         if (needsParens) {
689 |           add(")");
690 |         }
691 |         break;
692 |       }
693 | 
694 |       case Token.SWITCH:
695 |         add("switch(");
696 |         add(first);
697 |         add(")");
698 |         cc.beginBlock();
699 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
700 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
701 |         break;
702 | 
703 |       case Token.CASE:
704 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
705 |         add("case ");
706 |         add(first);
707 |         addCaseBody(last);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
711 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
712 |         add("default");
713 |         addCaseBody(first);
714 |         break;
715 | 
716 |       case Token.LABEL:
717 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
718 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
719 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
720 |         }
721 |         add(first);
722 |         add(":");
723 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
724 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
725 |         break;
726 | 
727 |       default:
728 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
732 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.computeVarList(Var,ReferenceCollection)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 268-307
Documentation:
/**
     * Computes a list of ever-referenced keys in the object being
     * inlined, and returns a mapping of key name -> generated
     * variable name.
     */
Method code:
268 |     private Map<String, String> computeVarList(
269 |         Var v, ReferenceCollection referenceInfo) {
270 |       Map<String, String> varmap = Maps.newLinkedHashMap();
271 | 
272 |       for (Reference ref : referenceInfo.references) {
273 |         if (ref.isLvalue() || ref.isInitializingDeclaration()) {
274 |           Node val = ref.getAssignedValue();
275 |           if (val != null) {
276 |             Preconditions.checkState(val.isObjectLit());
277 |             for (Node child = val.getFirstChild(); child != null;
278 |                  child = child.getNext()) {
279 |               String varname = child.getString();
280 |               if (varmap.containsKey(varname)) {
281 |                 continue;
282 |               }
283 | 
284 |               String var = VAR_PREFIX + varname + "_" +
285 |                 safeNameIdSupplier.get();
286 |               varmap.put(varname, var);
287 |             }
288 |           }
289 |         } else if (ref.getParent().isVar()) {
290 |           // This is the var. There is no value.
291 |         } else {
292 |           Node getprop = ref.getParent();
293 |           Preconditions.checkState(getprop.isGetProp());
294 | 
295 |           // The key being looked up in the original map.
296 |           String varname = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
297 |           if (varmap.containsKey(varname)) {
298 |             continue;
299 |           }
300 | 
301 |           String var = VAR_PREFIX + varname + "_" + safeNameIdSupplier.get();
302 |           varmap.put(varname, var);
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 | 
306 |       return varmap;
307 |     }

     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.

Method code:
414 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
415 |       Preconditions.checkState(
416 |           n.isName()
417 |           || n.isString()
418 |           || n.isStringKey()
419 |           || n.isGetterDef()

421 | 
422 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
423 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
424 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
425 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);

430 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
431 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&

440 |           }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }

===== M0322 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.isVarInlineForbidden(Var)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 129-145
Documentation:
/**
     * Whether the given variable is forbidden from being inlined.
     */
Method code:
129 |     private boolean isVarInlineForbidden(Var var) {
130 |       // A variable may not be inlined if:
131 |       // 1) The variable is defined in the externs
132 |       // 2) The variable is exported,
133 |       // 3) Don't inline the special RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME
134 |       // 4) A reference to the variable has been inlined. We're downstream
135 |       //    of the mechanism that creates variable references, so we don't
136 |       //    have a good way to update the reference. Just punt on it.
137 | 
138 |       // Additionally, exclude global variables for now.
139 | 
140 |       return var.isGlobal()
141 |           || var.isExtern()
142 |           || compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.name)
143 |           || RenameProperties.RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(var.name)
144 |           || staleVars.contains(var);
145 |     }

463 |       if (!name.equals(ARGUMENTS)) {
464 |         if (global) {

===== M0327 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 480-493
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
480 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
481 |       Node ret;
482 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
483 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
484 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
485 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
486 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
487 |       } else {
488 |         ret = transform(n);
489 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
490 |       }
491 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
492 |       return ret;
493 |     }

50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(

55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

===== M0341 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 212-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0351 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 489-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
489 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
490 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
491 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
492 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
493 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
494 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
495 |         String inputName = input.getName();
496 | 
497 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
498 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
499 |         // will get an error down the line.
500 |         inputs.add(input);
501 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
502 |       }
503 |     }
504 |     return inputs;
505 |   }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1323-1325
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1323 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1324 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1325 |   }

===== M0368 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

===== M0376 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 511-546
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
514 | 
515 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
516 | 
517 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
518 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
519 | 
520 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
521 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
522 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     curNode = n;
526 |     pushScope(n);
527 | 
528 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
529 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
530 |       // scope.
531 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
535 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
536 | 
537 |     // Args
538 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
539 | 
540 |     // Body
541 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
542 |             body.isBlock(), body);
543 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
544 | 
545 |     popScope();
546 |   }

/**
     * Do normalizations that introduce new siblings or parents.
     */
Method code:
498 |     private void doStatementNormalizations(
499 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
500 |       if (n.isLabel()) {
501 |         normalizeLabels(n);
502 |       }
503 | 
504 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs and LABELs, as all these
505 |       // are the only legal place for VARs and FOR statements.

513 |         splitVarDeclarations(n);
514 |       }
515 |

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-276
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (negativeZero) {
250 |       addConstant("-0");
251 |     } else if ((long) x == x) {
252 |       long value = (long) x;
253 |       long mantissa = value;
254 |       int exp = 0;
255 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
256 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
257 |           mantissa /= 10;
258 |           exp++;
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |       if (exp > 2) {
262 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
263 |       } else {
264 |         long valueAbs = Math.abs(value);
265 |         if (Long.toHexString(valueAbs).length() + 2 <
266 |             Long.toString(valueAbs).length()) {
267 |           addConstant((value < 0 ? "-" : "") + "0x" +
268 |               Long.toHexString(valueAbs));
269 |         } else {
270 |           addConstant(Long.toString(value));
271 |         }
272 |       }
273 |     } else {
274 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x).replace(".0E", "E"));
275 |     }
276 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
481 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {

483 |          current != null;

485 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {

489 |     return false;

120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {

137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:

150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:

153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:

161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:

167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {

217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.
   *
   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:
   * <ol>
   * <li>In CodingConventionAnnotator, any name that matches the
   *     {@link CodingConvention#isConstant(String)} is annotated with an
   *     IS_CONSTANT_NAME property.
   * <li>The normalize pass renames any variable with the IS_CONSTANT_NAME
   *     annotation and that is initialized to a constant value with
   *     a variable name including $$constant.
   * <li>Return true here if the variable includes $$constant in its name.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param node A NAME or STRING node
   * @return True if the variable is constant
   */
Method code:
2800 |   static boolean isConstantName(Node node) {
2801 |     return node.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
2802 |   }

773 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {

===== M0467 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 295-343
Documentation:

Method code:
295 |     @Override
296 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
297 |       if (n.isName()) {
298 |         String name = n.getString();
299 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
300 |           return;
301 |         }
302 | 
303 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
304 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
305 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
306 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
307 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
308 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
309 |             expectedConst = true;
310 |           } else {
311 |             expectedConst = false;
312 | 
313 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
314 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
315 |             if (var != null) {
316 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
317 |             }
318 | 
319 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
320 |               expectedConst = true;
321 |             } else {
322 |               expectedConst = false;
323 |             }
324 |           }
325 | 
326 |           if (expectedConst) {
327 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
328 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
329 |           } else {
330 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
331 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
332 |           }
333 |         }
334 | 
335 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
336 |         if (value == null) {
337 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
338 |         } else {
339 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
340 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
341 |         }
342 |       }
343 |     }

===== M0479 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the Javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

81 |       return false;
82 |     }

===== M0481 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 396-421
Documentation:

Method code:
396 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
397 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
398 |     // two cases:
399 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
400 |     // and
401 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
402 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
403 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
404 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
405 |     //
406 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
407 |     //
408 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
409 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
410 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
411 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
412 |     //
413 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
414 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
415 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
416 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
417 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
418 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
419 |     }
420 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
421 |   }

/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1168 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1169 |     switch (type) {
1170 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:

1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:

1180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:

1183 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1184 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1185 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1186 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1187 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1188 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;

1190 |       case Token.NE:

1196 |       case Token.GE:

1198 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;

1200 |       case Token.RSH:
1201 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1202 |       case Token.SUB:
1203 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;

1205 |       case Token.MOD:

===== M0500 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineObjectLiterals.InliningBehavior.splitObject(Var,Reference,Reference,ReferenceCollection)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineObjectLiterals.java
Original range: 396-467
Documentation:
/**
     * Splits up the object literal into individual variables, and
     * updates all uses.
     */
Method code:
396 |     private void splitObject(Var v, Reference declaration,
397 |                              Reference init,
398 |                              ReferenceCollection referenceInfo) {
399 |       // First figure out the FULL set of possible keys, so that they
400 |       // can all be properly set as necessary.
401 |       Map<String, String> varmap = computeVarList(v, referenceInfo);
402 | 
403 |       Map<String, Node> initvals = Maps.newHashMap();
404 |       // Figure out the top-level of the var assign node. If it's a plain
405 |       // ASSIGN, then there's an EXPR_STATEMENT above it, if it's a
406 |       // VAR then it should be directly replaced.
407 |       Node vnode;
408 |       boolean defined = referenceInfo.isWellDefined() &&
409 |           init.getParent().isVar();
410 |       if (defined) {
411 |         vnode = init.getParent();
412 |         fillInitialValues(init, initvals);
413 |       } else {
414 |         // TODO(user): More test / rewrite this part.
415 |         // Find the beginning of the function / script.
416 |         vnode = v.getScope().getRootNode().getLastChild().getFirstChild();
417 |       }
418 | 
419 |       for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : varmap.entrySet()) {
420 |         Node val = initvals.get(entry.getKey());
421 |         Node varnode = NodeUtil.newVarNode(entry.getValue(), val);
422 |         if (val == null) {
423 |           // is this right?
424 |           varnode.copyInformationFromForTree(vnode);
425 |         } else {
426 |           blacklistVarReferencesInTree(val, v.scope);
427 |         }
428 |         vnode.getParent().addChildBefore(varnode, vnode);
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (defined) {
432 |         vnode.getParent().removeChild(vnode);
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       for (Reference ref : referenceInfo.references) {
436 |         // The init/decl have already been converted.
437 |         if (defined && ref == init) continue;
438 | 
439 |         if (ref.isLvalue()) {
440 |           // Assignments have to be handled specially, since they
441 |           // expand out into multiple assignments.
442 |           replaceAssignmentExpression(v, ref, varmap);
443 |         } else if (ref.getParent().isVar()) {
444 |           // The old variable declaration. It didn't have a
445 |           // value. Remove it entirely as it should now be unused.
446 |           ref.getGrandparent().removeChild(ref.getParent());
447 |         } else {
448 |           // Make sure that the reference is a GETPROP as we expect it to be.
449 |           Node getprop = ref.getParent();
450 |           Preconditions.checkState(getprop.isGetProp());
451 | 
452 |           // The key being looked up in the original map.
453 |           String var = getprop.getChildAtIndex(1).getString();
454 | 
455 |           // If the variable hasn't already been declared, add an empty
456 |           // declaration near all the other declarations.
457 |           Preconditions.checkState(varmap.containsKey(var));
458 | 
459 |           // Replace the GETPROP node with a NAME.
460 |           Node replacement = IR.name(varmap.get(var));
461 |           replacement.copyInformationFrom(getprop);
462 |           ref.getGrandparent().replaceChild(ref.getParent(), replacement);
463 |         }
464 |       }
465 | 
466 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
467 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 647-685
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to JS code
   */
Method code:
647 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
648 |       CompilerOptions options, SourceMap sourceMap,  boolean tagAsStrict) {
649 |     Preconditions.checkState(options.sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
650 | 
651 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
652 |     Charset outputCharset = options.outputCharset == null ? null :
653 |         Charset.forName(options.outputCharset);
654 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
655 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
656 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
657 |             options.lineBreak,
658 |             options.preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile,
659 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,
660 |             createSourceMap,
661 |             options.sourceMapDetailLevel)
662 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
663 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,
664 |             createSourceMap,
665 |             options.sourceMapDetailLevel);
666 |     CodeGenerator cg =
667 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
668 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
669 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset, options.preferSingleQuotes);
670 | 
671 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
672 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
673 |     }
674 | 
675 |     cg.add(root);
676 |     mcp.endFile();
677 | 
678 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
679 | 
680 |     if (createSourceMap) {
681 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
682 |     }
683 | 
684 |     return code;
685 |   }

154 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
155 |     switch (n.getType()) {
156 |       // Childless expressions

172 |       case Token.VOID:
173 |       case Token.BITNOT:
174 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
175 |         return;
176 | 
177 |       // General binary ops
178 |       case Token.COMMA:
179 |       case Token.OR:
180 |       case Token.AND:
181 |       case Token.BITOR:

199 |       case Token.MUL:
200 |       case Token.MOD:
201 |       case Token.DIV:
202 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
203 |         return;
204 |

206 |       case Token.ASSIGN:

219 |         return;
220 |

226 |       case Token.STRING:

228 |         return;

232 |         return;

===== M0528 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 624-636
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
624 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
625 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
626 |     if (typeSet == null) {
627 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
628 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
632 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
633 | 
634 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
635 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
636 |   }

1283 | 
1284 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1285 |     // individual file parse trees.
1286 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1287 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1288 |     }
1289 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1290 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1291 |     }
1292 | 
1293 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1294 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1295 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1296 | 
1297 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1298 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1299 | 
1300 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1301 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1302 | 
1303 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1304 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1305 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1306 |     }
1307 | 
1308 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1309 | 
1310 |     try {
1311 |       // Parse externs sources.
1312 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1313 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1314 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1315 |           return null;
1316 |         }
1317 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1318 |       }
1319 | 
1320 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1321 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1322 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1323 |       }
1324 | 
1325 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1326 | 
1327 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1328 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1329 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1330 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1331 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1332 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1333 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1334 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1335 |           }
1336 |         }
1337 | 
1338 |         try {
1339 |           inputs =
1340 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1341 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1342 |           staleInputs = true;
1343 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1344 |           report(JSError.make(
1345 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1346 | 
1347 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1348 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1349 |             return null;
1350 |           }
1351 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1352 |           report(JSError.make(
1353 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1354 | 
1355 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1356 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1357 |             return null;
1358 |           }
1359 |         }
1360 |       }
1361 | 
1362 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1363 | 
1364 |       if (staleInputs) {
1365 |         repartitionInputs();
1366 |       }
1367 | 
1368 |       // Build the AST.
1369 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1370 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1371 |         if (n == null) {
1372 |           continue;
1373 |         }
1374 | 
1375 |         if (devMode) {
1376 |           runSanityCheck();
1377 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1378 |             return null;
1379 |           }
1380 |         }
1381 | 
1382 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1383 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1384 | 
1385 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1386 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1387 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1388 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1389 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1390 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1391 |         }
1392 | 
1393 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1394 |       }
1395 | 
1396 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1397 |         return null;
1398 |       }
1399 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1400 |     } finally {
1401 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1402 |     }
1403 |   }

===== M0539 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 541-556
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
541 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
542 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
543 |       Set<String> directives = null;
544 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
545 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
546 |         if (directives == null) {
547 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
548 |         } else {
549 |           directives.add(directive);
550 |         }
551 |       }
552 | 
553 |       if (directives != null) {
554 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
555 |       }
556 |     }

===== M0543 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 617-634
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
617 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
618 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
619 |         next = c.getNext();
620 |         if (c.isVar()) {
621 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
622 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
623 |           }
624 | 
625 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
626 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
627 |             c.removeChild(name);
628 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
629 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
630 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
631 |           }
632 |         }
633 |       }
634 |     }

===== M0547 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

===== M0576 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 501-521
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
501 |   @Override
502 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
503 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
504 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
505 |     if (unknown) {
506 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
507 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
508 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
509 |         unknown = false;
510 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
511 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
512 |             unknown = true;
513 |             break;
514 |           }
515 |         }
516 |       } else {
517 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |     return unknown;
521 |   }

233 |   private void declareVar(Node n) {
234 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isName());

Documentation:

Method code:
543 |   public Node getChildBefore(Node child) {
544 |     if (child == first) {
545 |       return null;
546 |     }
547 |     Node n = first;
548 |     while (n.next != child) {
549 |       n = n.next;
550 |       if (n == null) {
551 |         throw new RuntimeException("node is not a child");
552 |       }
553 |     }
554 |     return n;
555 |   }

Original range: 2719-2729
Documentation:
/**
   * A pre-order traversal, calling Visitor.visit for each child matching
   * the predicate.
   */
Method code:
2719 |   static void visitPreOrder(Node node,
2720 |                      Visitor visitor,
2721 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2722 |     visitor.visit(node);
2723 | 
2724 |     if (traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2725 |       for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2726 |         visitPreOrder(c, visitor, traverseChildrenPred);
2727 |       }
2728 |     }
2729 |   }

===== M0605 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.replaceChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 704-725
Documentation:
/**
   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
   */
Method code:
704 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
705 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
706 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
707 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
708 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
709 | 
710 |     // Copy over important information.
711 |     newChild.copyInformationFrom(child);
712 | 
713 |     newChild.next = child.next;
714 |     newChild.parent = this;
715 |     if (child == first) {
716 |         first = newChild;
717 |     } else {
718 |         Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
719 |         prev.next = newChild;
720 |     }
721 |     if (child == last)
722 |         last = newChild;
723 |     child.next = null;
724 |     child.parent = null;
725 |   }

===== M0607 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

===== M0615 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 464-484
Documentation:

Method code:
464 |     @Override
465 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
466 |       if (lineBreak) {
467 |         if (sawFunction) {
468 |           startNewLine();
469 |           sawFunction = false;
470 |         }
471 |       }
472 | 
473 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
474 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
475 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
476 |       int len = code.length();
477 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
478 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
479 |         if (ch == ';') {
480 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
481 |         }
482 |       }
483 |       maybeCutLine();
484 |     }
