===== M0622 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 75-96
Documentation:

Method code:
75 |   @Override
76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used, the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.
82 |       Preconditions.checkState(
83 |           !declarationRoot.isFunction() ||
84 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
85 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
86 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
87 |     } else {
88 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     if (!declarationRoot.isFunction()) {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

===== M0625 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 221-242
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 |   }

/**
   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.

Method code:
499 |   private static boolean mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node n) {
500 |     switch (n.getType()) {
501 |       case Token.EMPTY:
502 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
503 |         // EMPTY and FUNCTION are used both in expression and statement
504 |         // contexts
505 |         return true;
506 | 
507 |       case Token.BLOCK:
508 |       case Token.BREAK:
509 |       case Token.CONST:
510 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
511 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
512 |       case Token.DO:
513 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
514 |       case Token.FOR:
515 |       case Token.IF:
516 |       case Token.LABEL:
517 |       case Token.SWITCH:
518 |       case Token.THROW:
519 |       case Token.TRY:
520 |       case Token.VAR:
521 |       case Token.WHILE:
522 |       case Token.WITH:
523 |         return true;
524 | 
525 |       default:
526 |         return false;
527 |     }

179 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,

===== M0652 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 638-657
Documentation:

Method code:
638 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
639 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
640 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
641 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
642 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
643 |       if (typeSet == null) {
644 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
645 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
646 |       }
647 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
648 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
649 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
650 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
651 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
652 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
653 |       for (JSType alternate : type.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
654 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
655 |       }
656 |     }
657 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java

Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2406 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2407 |     int len = s.length();
2408 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2409 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2410 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2411 |         return false;
2412 |       }
2413 |     }
2414 |     return true;
2415 |   }

/**
     * Rewrite named unhoisted functions declarations to a known
     * consistent behavior so we don't to different logic paths for the same
     * code. From:
     *    function f() {}
     * to:
     *    var f = function () {};
     */

455 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());

458 |         rewriteFunctionDeclaration(n);

===== M0706 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

===== M0722 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 445-492
Documentation:

Method code:
445 |   @Override
446 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
447 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
448 |       return true;
449 |     }
450 | 
451 |     // Union types
452 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
453 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
454 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
455 |       return false;
456 |     }
457 | 
458 |     // record types
459 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
460 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     // Interfaces
464 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
465 |     // to the interface instance.
466 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
467 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
468 | 
469 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
470 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
471 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
472 |           return true;
473 |         }
474 |       }
475 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
476 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
477 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
478 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
479 |           return true;
480 |         }
481 |       }
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     // other prototype based objects
485 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
486 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
487 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
488 |       // to avoid guessing.
489 |       return true;
490 |     }
491 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
492 |   }

/**

     * between JsDoc on the object literal node and JsDoc on the object literal
     * value. For example,
     * <pre>
     * var x = {
     *   / JSDOC /

     *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'

===== M0726 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1408-1444
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1408 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1409 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1410 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1411 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1412 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1413 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1414 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1415 |           continue;
1416 |         }
1417 |       }
1418 | 
1419 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1420 | 
1421 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1422 |       if (n == null) {
1423 |         continue;
1424 |       }
1425 | 
1426 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1427 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1428 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1429 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1430 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1431 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1432 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1433 | 
1434 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1435 | 
1436 |         externs.add(input);
1437 |         staleInputs = true;
1438 |       }
1439 |     }
1440 | 
1441 |     if (staleInputs) {
1442 |       repartitionInputs();
1443 |     }
1444 |   }

===== M0728 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 1092-1128
Documentation:

Method code:
1092 |     @Override
1093 |     Node processUnaryExpression(UnaryExpression exprNode) {
1094 |       int type = transformTokenType(exprNode.getType());
1095 |       Node operand = transform(exprNode.getOperand());
1096 |       if (type == Token.NEG && operand.isNumber()) {
1097 |         operand.setDouble(-operand.getDouble());
1098 |         return operand;
1099 |       } else {
1100 |         if (type == Token.DELPROP &&
1101 |             !(operand.isGetProp() ||
1102 |               operand.isGetElem() ||
1103 |               operand.isName())) {
1104 |           String msg =
1105 |               "Invalid delete operand. Only properties can be deleted.";
1106 |           errorReporter.error(
1107 |               msg,
1108 |               sourceName,
1109 |               operand.getLineno(), "", 0);
1110 |         } else  if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1111 |           if (!validAssignmentTarget(operand)) {
1112 |             String msg = (type == Token.INC)
1113 |                 ? "invalid increment target"
1114 |                 : "invalid decrement target";
1115 |             errorReporter.error(
1116 |                 msg,
1117 |                 sourceName,
1118 |                 operand.getLineno(), "", 0);
1119 |           }
1120 |         }
1121 | 
1122 |         Node node = newNode(type, operand);
1123 |         if (exprNode.isPostfix()) {
1124 |           node.putBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP, true);
1125 |         }
1126 |         return node;
1127 |       }
1128 |     }

===== M0732 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.BasicBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 703-722
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether this block is guaranteed to begin executing before
     * the given block does.
     */
Method code:
703 |     boolean provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock thatBlock) {
704 |       // If thatBlock is a descendant of this block, and there are no hoisted
705 |       // blocks between them, then this block must start before thatBlock.
706 |       BasicBlock currentBlock;
707 |       for (currentBlock = thatBlock;
708 |            currentBlock != null && currentBlock != this;
709 |            currentBlock = currentBlock.getParent()) {
710 |         if (currentBlock.isHoisted) {
711 |           return false;
712 |         }
713 |       }
714 | 
715 |       if (currentBlock == this) {
716 |         return true;
717 |       }
718 |       if (isGlobalScopeBlock() && thatBlock.isGlobalScopeBlock()) {
719 |         return true;
720 |       }
721 |       return false;
722 |     }

===== M0735 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalVarReferenceMap.resetGlobalVarReferences(Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalVarReferenceMap.java
Original range: 78-87
Documentation:
/**
   * Resets global var reference map with the new provide map.
   *
   * @param globalRefMap The reference map result of a
   *     {@link ReferenceCollectingCallback} pass collected from the whole AST.
   */
Method code:
78 |   private void resetGlobalVarReferences(
79 |       Map<Var, ReferenceCollection> globalRefMap) {
80 |     refMap = Maps.newHashMap();
81 |     for (Entry<Var, ReferenceCollection> entry : globalRefMap.entrySet()) {
82 |       Var var = entry.getKey();
83 |       if (var.isGlobal()) {
84 |         refMap.put(var.getName(), entry.getValue());
85 |       }
86 |     }
87 |   }
